
FUNCTION '04_LOB' GLOBAL
    IF (Object['Claim']['Secondary']['LOB']['IsPresent'] = True) = True THEN
        IF Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsChangeNeeded'] = True THEN
            # make sure first word is updated in object for new LOB
            SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['Validated LOB']['First Word'] TO Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['NewInsName']
        ELSE
            SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['Validated LOB']['First Word'] TO Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['First Word']
        END
        IF Object['Claim']['Secondary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsChangeNeeded'] = True THEN
            # make sure first word is updated in object for new LOB
            SET Object['Claim']['Secondary']['Validated LOB']['First Word'] TO Object['Claim']['Secondary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['NewInsName']
        ELSE
            SET Object['Claim']['Secondary']['Validated LOB']['First Word'] TO Object['Claim']['Secondary']['LOB']['First Word']
        END
        CALL '10_Ins_Sequence'
    END
    **REGION For Primary LOB Check in Matrix
    **REGION convert boolean to string
    IF Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Final Info']['IsAttributed'] = True THEN
        SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Final Info']['IsAttributed'] TO $'''True'''
    ELSE
        SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Final Info']['IsAttributed'] TO $'''False'''
    END
    IF Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsMedicare'] = True THEN
        SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsMedicare'] TO $'''True'''
    ELSE
        SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsMedicare'] TO $'''False'''
    END
    SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['NewInsName'] TO $'''%''%'''
    **ENDREGION
    **REGION self learning LOB model
    Variables.ClearDataTable DataTable: InMatrix_DT_Filtered
    Variables.ClearDataTable DataTable: NotInMatrix_ExcelData_Filtered
    Variables.ClearDataTable DataTable: DefaultIns_ExcelData_Filtered
    Variables.ClearDataTable DataTable: InMatrixDataRow
    Variables.ClearDataTable DataTable: NotInMatrixDataRow
    Variables.ClearDataTable DataTable: DefaultInsDataRow
    IF Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsChangeNeeded'] = True THEN
        # if ins is different in eligibility for AARP UHC and PREFERRED
        Variables.FilterDataTable DataTable: InMatrix_ExcelData FilterParameters: { ^['RULE', 'COLUMN', 'OPERATOR', 'VALUE'], [$'''Or''', $'''Center''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Main Screen']['Facility']], [$'''And''', $'''First Word''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['NewInsName']], [$'''And''', $'''IsAttributed''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Final Info']['IsAttributed']], [$'''And''', $'''IsMedicare''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsMedicare']], [$'''And''', $'''Ins Type''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['Insurance Type']] } FilteredDataTable=> InMatrix_DT_Filtered
        IF InMatrix_DT_Filtered.RowsCount = 1 THEN
            # present in matrix
            SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['InMatrix'] TO True
            IF (InMatrix_DT_Filtered[0]['ECW ID'] <> Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['Ins Id']) = True THEN
                SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['IsChangeNeeded'] TO True
                SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['NewPayerID'] TO InMatrix_DT_Filtered[0]['ECW ID']
                SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['NewInsName'] TO InMatrix_DT_Filtered[0]['eCW Insurance']
            END
        ELSE
            Variables.FilterDataTable DataTable: NotInMatrix_ExcelData FilterParameters: { ^['RULE', 'COLUMN', 'OPERATOR', 'VALUE'], [$'''Or''', $'''Center''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Main Screen']['Facility']], [$'''And''', $'''First Word''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['NewInsName']], [$'''And''', $'''IsAttributed''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Final Info']['IsAttributed']], [$'''And''', $'''IsMedicare''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsMedicare']], [$'''And''', $'''Ins Type''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['Insurance Type']] } FilteredDataTable=> NotInMatrix_ExcelData_Filtered
            IF NotInMatrix_ExcelData_Filtered.RowsCount = 1 THEN
                # condition ins name miss match ins type miss match
                # lob not in matrix confirmed now check default insurance
                SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['InMatrix'] TO False
                Variables.FilterDataTable DataTable: DefaultIns_ExcelData FilterParameters: { ^['RULE', 'COLUMN', 'OPERATOR', 'VALUE'], [$'''Or''', $'''Center''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Main Screen']['Facility']], [$'''And''', $'''First Word''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['NewInsName']], [$'''And''', $'''IsAttributed''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Final Info']['IsAttributed']], [$'''And''', $'''IsMedicare''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsMedicare']], [$'''And''', $'''Ins Type''', $'''Equal''', Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['Insurance Type']] } FilteredDataTable=> DefaultIns_ExcelData_Filtered
                IF DefaultIns_ExcelData_Filtered.RowsCount = 1 THEN
                    # found default LOB
                    SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['IsChangeNeeded'] TO True
                    SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['NewPayerID'] TO DefaultIns_ExcelData_Filtered[0]['ECW ID']
                    SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['NewInsName'] TO DefaultIns_ExcelData_Filtered[0]['eCW Insurance']
                ELSE
                    # not found ask user for payer ID
                    Display.ShowMessageDialog.ShowMessageWithTimeout Title: $'''User Input👤''' Message: $'''👉Press OK!  ✅ 
👉Will be Auto Canceled!!!❌''' Icon: Display.Icon.Warning Buttons: Display.Buttons.OKCancel DefaultButton: Display.DefaultButton.Button2 IsTopMost: True Timeout: 30 ButtonPressed=> Button
                    SWITCH Button
                        CASE = $'''OK'''
                            @@statistics_TextBlock: '5'
@@statistics_Input_Text: '3'
@@statistics_Action_Submit: '2'
Display.ShowCustomDialog CardTemplateJson: '''{

      \"type\": \"Input.Text\",

      \"associatedInputs\": \"none\",
      \"title\": \"${Cancel_Title}\",
      \"IsCancel\": true

}''' CustomFormData=> CustomFormData ButtonPressed=> ButtonPressed @AdaptiveCard_FormTitle: $'''Default LOB👤''' @Text_block_Text: $'''Semi Self Learning Algorithm...............✅''' @Text_block5_Text: $'''👉Current Insurance Type:''' @Text_block7_Text: $'''✔New Ins Name : %Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['NewInsName']%✅''' @Text_block6_Text: $'''✔Ins Type : %Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['Insurance Type']%✅''' @Text_block8_Text: $'''✔️Facility: %Object['Claim']['Main Screen']['Facility']% ✅''' @NewPayerID_Label: $'''👉Enter New Payer ID:''' @NewPayerID_ErrorMessage: $'''Invaid Value ⚠️''' @NewPayerID_Regex: $'''^\\d{4}$''' @NewLOBName_Label: $'''👉Enter Full LOB Name:''' @NewLOBName_ErrorMessage: $'''Invalid ⚠️''' @NewLOBName_Regex: $'''^[A-Z]+(?: [A-Z]+)*$''' @NewLOBFirstWord_Label: $'''👉Enter LOB First Word:''' @NewLOBFirstWord_Regex: $'''^[A-Z]+$''' @OK_Title: $'''OK''' @Cancel_Title: $'''Cancel'''
                            SWITCH ButtonPressed
                                CASE = $'''OK'''
                                    SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['IsChangeNeeded'] TO True
                                    SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['NewPayerID'] TO CustomFormData['NewPayerID']
                                    SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['NewInsName'] TO CustomFormData['NewLOBName']
                                    Variables.CreateNewDatatable InputTable: { ^['Center', 'ECW ID', 'eCW Insurance', 'eCW Product Description', 'First Word', 'IsAttributed', 'IsMedicare', 'Ins Type', 'Taught By', 'Date Added'], [Object['Claim']['Main Screen']['Facility'], CustomFormData['NewPayerID'], CustomFormData['NewLOBName'], $'''''', CustomFormData['NewLOBFirstWord'], Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Final Info']['IsAttributed'], Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsMedicare'], Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['Insurance Type'], Path_DataTable[5][1], CurrentDateTime] } DataTable=> DefaultInsDataRow
                                    Excel.LaunchExcel.LaunchAndOpenUnderExistingProcess Path: Path_DataTable[3][1] Visible: False ReadOnly: False Instance=> LOB_Excel
                                    Excel.SetActiveWorksheet.ActivateWorksheetByName Instance: LOB_Excel Name: $'''Default'''
                                    Excel.GetFirstFreeColumnRow Instance: LOB_Excel FirstFreeColumn=> FirstFreeColumn_in_LOB FirstFreeRow=> FirstFreeRow_in_LOB
                                    Excel.WriteToExcel.WriteCell Instance: LOB_Excel Value: DefaultInsDataRow Column: $'''A''' Row: FirstFreeRow_in_LOB
                                    Excel.GetFirstFreeColumnRow Instance: LOB_Excel FirstFreeColumn=> FirstFreeColumn_in_LOB FirstFreeRow=> FirstFreeRow_in_LOB
                                    Excel.ReadFromExcel.ReadCells Instance: LOB_Excel StartColumn: $'''A''' StartRow: 1 EndColumn: FirstFreeColumn_in_LOB - 1 EndRow: FirstFreeRow_in_LOB - 1 ReadAsText: False FirstLineIsHeader: True RangeValue=> DefaultIns_ExcelData
                                    Excel.CloseExcel.CloseAndSave Instance: LOB_Excel
                                CASE = $'''Cancel'''
                                    /# go directly to next claim and all object variable must be reset and close the claim by
pressing cancel button#/
                                    WebAutomation.PressButton.PressButtonCloseDialog BrowserInstance: edgeBrowser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Lookup.jsp\' 2']['claim main screen cancel button'] WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                                    NEXT LOOP
                            END
                        CASE = $'''Cancel'''
                            /# go directly to next claim and all object variable must be reset and close the claim by
pressing cancel button#/
                            WebAutomation.PressButton.PressButtonCloseDialog BrowserInstance: edgeBrowser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... Lookup.jsp\' 2']['claim main screen cancel button'] WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                            NEXT LOOP
                    END
                END
            ELSE
                # user form with payer name ID and first word
                Display.ShowMessageDialog.ShowMessageWithTimeout Title: $'''User Input👤''' Message: $'''👉Press OK!  ✅ 
👉Will be Auto Canceled!!!❌''' Icon: Display.Icon.Warning Buttons: Display.Buttons.OKCancel DefaultButton: Display.DefaultButton.Button2 IsTopMost: True Timeout: 30 ButtonPressed=> Button
                SWITCH Button
                    CASE = $'''OK'''
                        @@statistics_TextBlock: '8'
@@statistics_Input_ChoiceSet: '1'
@@statistics_Input_Text: '3'
@@statistics_Action_Submit: '2'

                                    Excel.CloseExcel.CloseAndSave Instance: LOB_Excel
                                ELSE
                                    # update lob not in matrix
                                    SET Object['Claim']['Primary']['LOB']['InMatrix'] TO False
                                    Variables.CreateNewDatatable InputTable: { ^['Center', 'ECW ID', 'eCW Insurance', 'eCW Product Description', 'First Word', 'IsAttributed', 'IsMedicare', 'Ins Type', 'Taught By', 'Date Added'], [Object['Claim']['Main Screen']['Facility'], CustomFormData['NewPayerID'], CustomFormData['NewLOBName'], $'''''', CustomFormData['NewLOBFirstWord'], Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Final Info']['IsAttributed'], Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['IsMedicare'], Object['Claim']['Primary']['Eligibility']['Ins Type Info']['Insurance Type'], Path_DataTable[5][1], CurrentDateTime] } DataTable=> NotInMatrixDataRow

            WebAutomation.ExecuteJavascript BrowserInstance: edgeBrowser Javascript: $'''function Claim_InLOB() {
    const getValue = (selector) => document.querySelector(selector)?.value || \"\";

    const setValue = (selector, value) => {
        const el = document.querySelector(selector);
        if (el) {
            el.focus();
            el.value = value;
            el.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent(\"keydown\", { bubbles: true }));
            el.dispatchEvent(new Event(\"input\", { bubbles: true }));
            el.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent(\"keyup\", { bubbles: true }));
            el.dispatchEvent(new Event(\"change\", { bubbles: true }));
        }
    };

    const cleanValue = (val) => val.replace(/[._-]/g, \"\").toUpperCase();

    // Clean and update subscriber number
    const subNoRaw = getValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvSN\"]\');
    const cleanedSubNo = cleanValue(subNoRaw);
    setValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvSN\"]\', cleanedSubNo);

    // Clean and update group number
    const groupNoRaw = getValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvGrpNo\"]\');
    const cleanedGroupNo = cleanValue(groupNoRaw);
    setValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvGrpNo\"]\', cleanedGroupNo);

    // Get name from td > h4
    const nameElement = document.querySelector(\'td#addressTd_insdetail h4\');
    let name = \"\";
    let firstWord = \"\";

    if (nameElement) {
        name = nameElement.textContent.trim().replace(/\\./g, \"\");
        firstWord = name.split(\" \")[0].toUpperCase();
    }

    // Get other values
    let sourceOfPayment = getValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvSOP\"]\');
    let insuranceClass = getValue(\'input[id=\"billingClaimInsuranceDetailsIpt4\"]\');
    const assignmentOfBenefits = getValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvAOB\"]\');
    let patientRel = getValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvPRTI\"]\');

    // Auto-fill Source of Payment and Insurance Class if empty
    if (!sourceOfPayment || !insuranceClass) {
        const upperName = name.toUpperCase();
        if ((upperName.includes(\"MCR ADV HMO\") || upperName.includes(\"RISK CAP\")) &&
            !upperName.includes(\"MEDICAID\") && !upperName.includes(\"MEDICARE\")) {
            sourceOfPayment = \"16\";
            insuranceClass = \"MCRADV\";
        } else if ((upperName.includes(\"MCR ADV PPO\") || upperName.includes(\"FFS\")) &&
            !upperName.includes(\"MEDICAID\") && !upperName.includes(\"MEDICARE\")) {
            sourceOfPayment = \"CI\";
            insuranceClass = \"FFS\";
        } else if (upperName.startsWith(\"MEDICARE\")) {
            sourceOfPayment = \"MB\";
            insuranceClass = \"FFS\";
        } else if (upperName.startsWith(\"MEDICAID\")) {
            sourceOfPayment = \"MC\";
            insuranceClass = \"FFS\";
        } else {
            sourceOfPayment = \"CI\";
            insuranceClass = \"FFS\";
        }

        setValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvSOP\"]\', sourceOfPayment);
        setValue(\'input[id=\"billingClaimInsuranceDetailsIpt4\"]\', insuranceClass);
    }

    // Ensure assignmentOfBenefits is \"Y\"
    if (assignmentOfBenefits !== \"Y\") {
        setValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvAOB\"]\', \"Y\");
    }

    // Ensure patientRel is \"1\"
    if (patientRel !== \"1\") {
        setValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvPRTI\"]\', \"1\");
        patientRel = \"1\";
    }

    // Determine if subNo needs correction
    let IssubNoCorrectionNeeded = false;

    if (cleanedSubNo.length <= 4) {
        IssubNoCorrectionNeeded = true;
    }  else if (firstWord === \"HUMANA\" && cleanedSubNo.startsWith(\"H\")) {
        if (cleanedSubNo.length < 11) {
            const padded = cleanedSubNo.padEnd(11, \"0\");
            setValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvSN\"]\', padded);
        }
    } else if (firstWord === \"CAREPLUS\") {
        if (cleanedSubNo.length === 7) {
            setValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvSN\"]\', cleanedSubNo + \"01\");
        } else if (cleanedSubNo.length === 9 && cleanedSubNo.endsWith(\"00\")) {
            const updated = cleanedSubNo.slice(0, 7) + \"01\";
            setValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvSN\"]\', updated);
        }
    }

    // Build final data object
    const data = {
        primaryCheckBox: document.querySelector(\'input[id=\"billingClaimInsuranceDetailsIpt1\"]\')?.checked || false,
        secondaryCheckBox: document.querySelector(\'input[id=\"billingClaimInsuranceDetailsIpt2\"]\')?.checked || false,
        tertiaryCheckBox: document.querySelector(\'input[id=\"billingClaimInsuranceDetailsIpt3\"]\')?.checked || false,

        sourceOfPayment: sourceOfPayment,
        insuranceClass: insuranceClass,
        claimFilingIndicator: getValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvCFI\"]\'),
        payerID: getValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvPayer\"]\'),
        subNo: getValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvSN\"]\'),
        insuredName: getValue(\'input[id=\"billingClaimInsuranceDetailsIpt5\"]\'),
        groupNo: getValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvGrpNo\"]\'),
        patientRel: patientRel,
        assignmentOfBenefits: getValue(\'input[id=\"billingInvAOB\"]\'),

        name: name,
        firstWord: firstWord,
        IssubNoCorrectionNeeded: IssubNoCorrectionNeeded
    };

    return JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);
}
